PART 2: AUTO FUNCTIONING
(from *Auto functioning.txt)
================================================================================

  Section 1: PREAMBLE (from *Auto functioning.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Final Architecture: A → (B + C) Hybrid

You build this in layers, not all at once.

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 2: Layer 4 — Safety Governor + Kill Switch (from *Auto functioning.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

🔹 Layer 0 — Productivity Core (Foundation)

Capabilities:

Email drafting + sending (approval required)

Calendar management

File search

Task creation

App launching

This becomes your stable base layer.

No autonomy yet.
Only structured action execution.

🔹 Layer 1 — Research Engine (B)

This is your intelligence amplifier.

Pipeline:

Search → Crawl → Extract → Reason → Summarize → Store

Capabilities:

Multi-source web research

Source credibility ranking

Fact comparison

Knowledge graph storage

Structured report generation

It should output:

Executive summary

Bullet findings

Source list

Confidence score

No direct OS control needed here.

🔹 Layer 2 — Controlled Desktop Operator (C)

This is where it acts.

But with strict rules.

It may:

Open apps

Navigate websites

Fill forms

Extract data from pages

Execute predefined workflows

It may NOT:

Run arbitrary shell commands

Delete files

Modify system settings

Install software

All actions must pass through:

Intent → Validator → Permission Check → Execution → Log
🔹 Layer 3 — Audit & Summary Engine (Your Critical Requirement)

This is extremely important.

After ANY autonomous workflow, the system must generate:

1️⃣ Action Log

Chronological steps taken.

2️⃣ Outcome Summary

What changed? What was accomplished?

3️⃣ Risk Flag

Anything unusual detected?

4️⃣ Optional Rollback Info

What could be undone?

Example Output

After task:

“Research best AI GPU under $2000 and draft comparison email.”

It returns:

🔍 Task Summary

Objective: Find best AI GPU under $2000 and draft comparison email.

Actions Taken:

Searched 5 sources via Bing API

Crawled NVIDIA, AMD official pages

Extracted benchmark data

Compared CUDA cores, VRAM, price/performance

Drafted email (not sent — awaiting approval)

Key Findings:

RTX 4080 Super — best CUDA performance

RX 7900 XTX — better VRAM per dollar

Used 4090 — best raw power but risky

Confidence: 0.82

No unsafe actions executed.

That summary engine gives you transparency.

This is mandatory for C.

🔹 Layer 4 — Safety Governor

You need:

1️⃣ Global Kill Switch

Hotkey + UI toggle.

2️⃣ Execution Watchdog

Every action checks:

if allow_execution == False:
    abort
3️⃣ Rate Limiter

Max X actions per minute.

4️⃣ Action Scope Restriction

It cannot operate outside declared task scope.

If task is:

“Research GPUs”

It cannot suddenly open your banking app.

The Brain Upgrade Required

You will need to extend your existing AI Girl brain (from 

TECHNICAL_ANALYSIS

) with:

New modules:

server/
 ├── planner.py
 ├── task_manager.py
 ├── action_auditor.py
 ├── safety_governor.py
 ├── workflow_engine.py
Internal Multi-Agent Design (Recommended)

Instead of one LLM call:

Use role separation:

Planner → Executor → Auditor → Reporter

Example:

User Request
   ↓
Planner creates step plan
   ↓
Executor executes structured actions
   ↓
Auditor verifies actions
   ↓
Reporter generates summary

This prevents chaos.

The Most Important Design Rule

Autonomy must be:

Explicitly scoped

Time bounded

Action bounded

Logged

Reversible when possible

Otherwise C becomes dangerous.

The System You’re Building Is Essentially

A local AI Operating Layer

Research Engine

Action Executor

Transparent Audit Log

🧠 Execution Flow (Exact Logic)
User gives task
↓
Planner generates full step plan
↓
System shows you:
  - Goal
  - Planned steps
  - Estimated risk level
↓
You approve once
↓
Execution begins
↓
Each action logged
↓
Auditor verifies
↓
Final structured summary returned
1️⃣ Planner Module (New)

Creates a structured plan.

Example input:

“Research best GPU under $2000 and prepare comparison email”

Planner output:

{
  "goal": "Find best GPU under $2000 and draft email",
  "steps": [
    {"type": "web_search", "query": "best AI GPU under 2000 2026"},
    {"type": "crawl", "target": "top 5 results"},
    {"type": "extract", "fields": ["price", "VRAM", "CUDA cores", "benchmarks"]},
    {"type": "compare"},
    {"type": "draft_email"},
    {"type": "open_gmail"}
  ],
  "risk_level": "LOW",
  "requires_desktop_control": true
}

You approve this once.

2️⃣ Execution Engine

Runs steps sequentially.

Every action must pass:

if action.type not in ALLOWED_ACTIONS:
    abort()

Actions allowed at Level 2:

open_app

open_url

web_search

crawl

extract

draft_email

fill_form (limited scope)

read_email

Not allowed:

shell_command

delete_file

install_software

system_settings

3️⃣ Scoped Autonomy Rule (Critical)

The system may ONLY execute actions declared in the approved plan.

If new action appears:
→ pause
→ request new approval

No scope drift allowed.

4️⃣ Audit & Summary Engine (Mandatory)

After execution:

You receive structured report:

🔎 TASK SUMMARY

Goal: Find best GPU under $2000

Execution Time: 2m 14s
Actions Executed: 9

Actions Performed:

Opened Chrome

Searched Bing API

Crawled 5 websites

Extracted benchmark tables

Compared 12 GPUs

Drafted email (not sent)

Results:

Best overall: RTX 4080 Super

Best VRAM value: RX 7900 XTX

Used option: RTX 4090 (higher risk)

Safety Report:

No restricted actions attempted

No scope violations detected

No file modifications

Confidence Score:

0.84

That summary makes Level 2 safe.

5️⃣ Safety Governor (Non-Negotiable)

You need:

Global Kill Switch

Keyboard hotkey.

Execution Timeout

If task exceeds X minutes → auto pause.

Action Rate Limit

Max N actions per minute.

Logging

Every action stored in:

/logs/task_YYYYMMDD_HHMM.json
6️⃣ System Modules to Add

Add these to your current Python brain:

server/
 ├── planner.py
 ├── executor.py
 ├── auditor.py
 ├── safety_governor.py
 ├── task_log.py
7️⃣ Research Layer (B Integration)

For research tasks:

Search API
↓
Crawler (Playwright or requests + bs4)
↓
Extractor (LLM structured output)
↓
Reasoning engine
↓
Report generator

Store extracted knowledge in:

SQLite (structured)

Vector DB (semantic search)

8️⃣ Desktop Operator (C Integration)

Use:

Playwright (browser control)

pyautogui (only if necessary, and sandboxed)

OS-specific app launch commands

Never allow raw shell execution.

What You Now Have

You are building:

A scoped autonomous AI execution engine
with research intelligence
desktop control
and mandatory transparency.

This is effectively a controlled AI operating layer.

🧠 Final System Architecture (Hybrid Level 2)
User Task
   ↓
Planner (Cloud LLM)
   ↓
Plan Preview + Risk Assessment
   ↓
Your Approval (Single confirmation)
   ↓
Local Executor (Python)
   ↓
Auditor (Local)
   ↓

   ↓
Final Report + Logs
🔹 Component Breakdown
1️⃣ Cloud Layer (Intelligence Layer)

Runs in cloud:

Planning

Multi-step reasoning

Structured extraction

Summary writing

Research synthesis

Email drafting

Uses:

GPT (cloud API)

Optional embedding API for semantic memory

This layer NEVER touches your OS.

It only outputs structured JSON.

2️⃣ Local Layer (Control Layer)

Runs on your machine:

App launching

Browser control

File access

Email sending (via API)

Logging

Kill switch

Scope enforcement

Action validation

This layer NEVER trusts raw LLM output blindly.

🔒 Safety Contract Between Layers

Cloud can suggest:

{
  "type": "open_app",
  "parameters": { "name": "Chrome" }
}

Local layer must verify:

if action.type not in ALLOWED_ACTIONS:
    reject()

if action not in approved_plan:
    pause_and_request_confirmation()

Cloud proposes.
Local enforces.

⚠️ Critical Constraint

Your system must:

Never execute shell commands directly from LLM

Never operate outside approved plan scope

Never self-modify code

Never escalate privileges

If it violates those rules, architecture is wrong.

🧠 Storage Architecture (Hybrid B Model)

You will have two data layers:

[ LOCAL MACHINE ]  →  Primary storage + execution truth
[ CLOUD STORAGE ]  →  Backup + analytics + long-term intelligence

Local is always authoritative.
Cloud is read-only backup + analysis layer.

🔹 What Stays Local (Primary Truth)

These must never depend on cloud availability:

1️⃣ Task Logs

Every action executed.

/logs/task_YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS.json
2️⃣ Execution History

SQLite database:

tasks

actions

safety flags

timestamps

outcome status

3️⃣ Memory Store

User-specific:

preferences

patterns

workflow habits

4️⃣ Permission Matrix

Never stored remotely.

5️⃣ Safety Flags & Kill Switch State

If internet dies → system still works safely.

🔹 What Goes to Cloud (Backup + Intelligence)

Cloud receives:

Task summaries

Research reports

Vector embeddings (for semantic recall)

Analytics metrics (optional)

Long-term compressed logs

Cloud NEVER receives:

Raw keystrokes

Screen captures

Local file content (unless explicitly requested)

System paths

OS-level secrets

🔐 Data Flow Design

After task completion:

Local Execution
↓
Local Auditor
↓
Local Log File Created
↓
Summary Generated
↓
Encrypted Upload to Cloud
↓
Cloud stores:
   - Summary
   - Metadata
   - Embeddings
🔒 Security Requirement (Non-Negotiable)

All cloud backups must be:

Encrypted before upload

Signed with local device key

Versioned

Example:

encrypted_blob = encrypt(summary_json, device_private_key)
upload(encrypted_blob)

Even if cloud compromised → raw logs unreadable.

🗂 Database Layout
Local SQLite

Tables:

tasks
actions
audit_reports
memory
workflow_templates
Cloud Database

Tables:

task_summaries
research_reports
analytics_metrics
vector_index

Cloud never stores raw action sequences.

Only compressed structured summaries.

You get:

Scalable research recall

Analytics potential

And you avoid:

Cloud dependency failures

Sensitive data exposure

🔐 Identity & Key Management (Critical for Commercialization)

Each installation must generate:

Device ID

Public/private key pair

Encrypted local storage

Flow:

Install App
↓
Generate device keypair
↓
Register device public key with cloud
↓
All backups encrypted with device key

Even your own company cannot read user logs without their key.

That is your competitive moat.

🧠 Multi-Tenant Design

Design cloud from start as:

User table

Device table

Rate limits per user

Never design it as single-user and “refactor later”.
That always fails.

🏗 Cross-Platform Architecture (Clean Separation)
UI Layer (Electron / Tauri)
        ↓
Core Engine (Platform-agnostic Python)
        ↓
OS Adapter Layer (macOS / Windows / Linux)

Only the OS Adapter knows about platform differences.

Everything else is identical.

🔹 1️⃣ Desktop Shell (UI Layer)

Use one of:

Option A — Electron (Most mature)

Node + Chromium

Massive ecosystem

Heavier but stable

Option B — Tauri (Modern, lighter)

Rust backend

Smaller bundle size

More complex tooling

For speed and ecosystem: Electron is safer for MVP.

It runs on:

macOS

Windows

Linux

🔹 2️⃣ Core Engine (Shared Across All OS)

Written in Python.

Contains:

Planner (cloud)

Executor logic

Safety governor

Auditor

Logger

Summary engine

Scope enforcer

This code NEVER checks if mac or windows.

It calls:

os_adapter.open_app("Chrome")

That’s it.

🔹 3️⃣ OS Adapter Layer (Critical Design)

Create:

os_adapters/
 ├── base_adapter.py
 ├── mac_adapter.py
 ├── windows_adapter.py
 ├── linux_adapter.py
base_adapter.py
class BaseAdapter:
    def open_app(self, name): pass
    def open_url(self, url): pass
    def get_active_window(self): pass
    def list_installed_apps(self): pass
mac_adapter.py
import subprocess

class MacAdapter(BaseAdapter):
    def open_app(self, name):
        subprocess.Popen(["open", "-a", name])
windows_adapter.py
import subprocess

class WindowsAdapter(BaseAdapter):
    def open_app(self, name):
        subprocess.Popen(["start", name], shell=True)
linux_adapter.py
import subprocess

class LinuxAdapter(BaseAdapter):
    def open_app(self, name):
        subprocess.Popen([name])
🔹 4️⃣ Platform Detection

On startup:

import platform

def get_adapter():
    os_name = platform.system()

    if os_name == "Darwin":
        return MacAdapter()
    elif os_name == "Windows":
        return WindowsAdapter()
    elif os_name == "Linux":
        return LinuxAdapter()
    else:
        raise Exception("Unsupported OS")

Now everything becomes modular.

🔐 Cross-Platform Security Constraints

Different OS = different risks.

macOS

Gatekeeper

Accessibility permissions required

Tighter app sandboxing

Windows

UAC prompts

PowerShell access risks

Linux

Environment fragmentation

Different desktop environments

Your rule:
No raw shell execution from LLM.
Only structured adapter calls.

🔹 5️⃣ Browser Automation (Cross-Platform Safe Choice)

Use:

Playwright

Playwright runs on:

macOS

Windows

Linux

It isolates browser control and avoids raw mouse automation.

Avoid pyautogui unless absolutely necessary.

🔹 6️⃣ Packaging Strategy

For consumer distribution:

macOS

.dmg installer

Signed + notarized

Windows

.exe installer

Code signing certificate

Linux

AppImage or .deb

Electron supports this via:

electron-builder

🔹 7️⃣ Update Mechanism

Must support:

Auto-updates

Rollback versioning

Signed update packages

Consumers expect seamless updates.

🔹 8️⃣ Cross-Platform Testing Requirement

You must test:

App launch behavior

Permission prompts

Browser automation

File access sandbox

Kill switch

On all three OS before release.

No shortcuts.

🚨 Biggest Mistake Teams Make

They write:

if platform == "mac":
    ...
elif platform == "windows":
    ...

Inside core logic everywhere.

That becomes unmaintainable.

All OS logic belongs in adapters only.

🧩 Module Layout

Includes:

Step sequence

    {"type": "draft_email"}

  "risk_level": "LOW",
  "requires_desktop_control": true
}
2️⃣ Local Executor

Executes approved plan step by step.

Validates every action against:

Allowed actions list (Phase 1)

Approved plan

Runs Playwright or equivalent for browser automation.

Logs action start/end, errors, exceptions.

Confidence score

Ensures transparency & user trust before Phase 2.

Action rate limiter

6️⃣ Storage

Local: SQLite database (tasks, actions, audit logs)

Cloud (optional): Encrypted summaries & embeddings only

Local always authoritative

Cloud is read-only backup + intelligence

  Section 19: PREAMBLE (from *AutomateAgents.txt)

"""
agent_scanner.py
────────────────
AST-based agent auto-discovery. Zero imports of user code — pure static analysis.

Detection strategies (all run on every scan):

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 20: 3. DECORATOR    — @agent, @swarm_agent, @register_agent on class or function (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Returns a flat list of AgentRecord dicts ready for the registry.
"""

import ast
import os
import hashlib
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass, field, asdict
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 21: # Root path to scan. Override via env or pass directly to Scanner. (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

DEFAULT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]  # project root

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 22: # Folder names treated as "agent directories" for strategy 2 (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

AGENT_FOLDER_NAMES = {"agents", "agent", "swarm", "cognitive", "cognitive_agents"}

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 23: # Base-class name patterns (case-insensitive substring match) (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

AGENT_BASE_PATTERNS = [
    r".*agent.*",
    r".*swarm.*node.*",
    r".*cognitive.*module.*",
]

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 24: # Decorator name patterns (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

AGENT_DECORATOR_PATTERNS = [
    "agent",
    "swarm_agent",
    "register_agent",
    "cognitive_agent",
    "aariya_agent",
]

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 25: # Directories to always skip (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

SKIP_DIRS = {
    "__pycache__", ".git", ".venv", "venv", "node_modules",
    "dist", "build", ".mypy_cache", ".pytest_cache",
}

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 26: # ── Data model ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@dataclass
class AgentRecord:
    id: str                          # stable unique key  (file_hash:class_name)
    name: str                        # class / function name
    file: str                        # relative path from project root
    line: int                        # line number of definition
    kind: str                        # "class" | "function"
    detection: list[str]             # which strategies matched
    base_classes: list[str]          # for inheritance strategy
    decorators: list[str]            # raw decorator names found
    docstring: Optional[str]         # first docstring line
    file_hash: str                   # sha1 of file content (change detection)
    scanned_at: str                  # ISO timestamp

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return asdict(self)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 27: # ── Core Scanner ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class AgentScanner:
    """
    Walk the project tree and return all discovered AgentRecords.

    Usage:
        scanner = AgentScanner(root="/path/to/project")
        records = scanner.scan()
    """

    def __init__(self, root: str | Path = DEFAULT_ROOT):
        self.root = Path(root).resolve()

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 28: # ── Public ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def scan(self) -> list[dict]:
        """Full scan. Returns list of AgentRecord dicts."""
        records: dict[str, AgentRecord] = {}

        for py_file in self._iter_python_files():
            try:
                file_records = self._scan_file(py_file)
                for rec in file_records:


    def update_from_scan(self, scan_results: list[dict]) -> dict:
        """
        Merge a fresh scan into the registry.
        Returns a diff object describing what changed.
        """
        with self._lock:
            now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
            scanned_ids = {r["id"] for r in scan_results}
            existing_ids = set(self._data["agents"].keys())

            diff = {"new": [], "updated": [], "removed": []}



    def start(self, loop: asyncio.AbstractEventLoop):
        """Call from FastAPI lifespan startup."""
        self.broadcaster.set_loop(loop)
        self._running = True

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 29: # Merge detections if same id appears in multiple strategies (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

if rec.id in records:
                        records[rec.id].detection = list(
                            set(records[rec.id].detection + rec.detection)
                        )
                    else:
                        records[rec.id] = rec
            except (SyntaxError, UnicodeDecodeError):
                pass  # skip unparseable files silently

        return [r.to_dict() for r in records.values()]

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 30: # ── Private helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _iter_python_files(self):
        for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(self.root):

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 31: # Prune skip dirs in-place (affects os.walk recursion) (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

dirnames[:] = [d for d in dirnames if d not in SKIP_DIRS]
            for fname in filenames:
                if fname.endswith(".py"):
                    yield Path(dirpath) / fname

    def _scan_file(self, path: Path) -> list[AgentRecord]:
        source = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
        tree = ast.parse(source, filename=str(path))
        file_hash = hashlib.sha1(source.encode()).hexdigest()[:12]
        rel_path = str(path.relative_to(self.root))
        in_agent_folder = self._is_in_agent_folder(path)

        records = []
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

        for node in ast.walk(tree):
            if isinstance(node, (ast.ClassDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef, ast.FunctionDef)):
                detections = []
                base_classes = []
                decorators = []

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 32: # Strategy 1 — inheritance (classes only) (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

if isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef):
                    bases = self._extract_base_names(node)
                    matched = [b for b in bases if self._matches_agent_base(b)]
                    if matched:
                        detections.append("inheritance")
                        base_classes = bases

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 33: # Strategy 2 — folder location (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

if in_agent_folder:
                    detections.append("folder")

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 34: # Strategy 3 — decorator (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

decs = self._extract_decorator_names(node)
                matched_decs = [d for d in decs if d.lower() in AGENT_DECORATOR_PATTERNS]
                if matched_decs:
                    detections.append("decorator")
                    decorators = decs

                if not detections:
                    continue  # not an agent

                kind = "class" if isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef) else "function"
                docstring = ast.get_docstring(node)
                doc_first_line = docstring.split("\n")[0].strip() if docstring else None

                stable_id = f"{file_hash}:{node.name}"

                records.append(AgentRecord(
                    id=stable_id,
                    name=node.name,
                    file=rel_path,
                    line=node.lineno,
                    kind=kind,
                    detection=detections,
                    base_classes=base_classes,
                    decorators=decorators,
                    docstring=doc_first_line,
                    file_hash=file_hash,
                    scanned_at=now,
                ))

        return records

    def _is_in_agent_folder(self, path: Path) -> bool:
        return any(part.lower() in AGENT_FOLDER_NAMES for part in path.parts)

    def _extract_base_names(self, node: ast.ClassDef) -> list[str]:
        names = []
        for base in node.bases:
            if isinstance(base, ast.Name):
                names.append(base.id)
            elif isinstance(base, ast.Attribute):
                names.append(f"{base.value.id}.{base.attr}" if isinstance(base.value, ast.Name) else base.attr)
        return names

    def _extract_decorator_names(self, node) -> list[str]:
        names = []
        for dec in node.decorator_list:
            if isinstance(dec, ast.Name):
                names.append(dec.id)
            elif isinstance(dec, ast.Attribute):
                names.append(dec.attr)
            elif isinstance(dec, ast.Call):
                func = dec.func
                if isinstance(func, ast.Name):
                    names.append(func.id)
                elif isinstance(func, ast.Attribute):
                    names.append(func.attr)
        return names

    def _matches_agent_base(self, name: str) -> bool:
        name_lower = name.lower()
        return any(re.match(pat, name_lower) for pat in AGENT_BASE_PATTERNS)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 35: # ── CLI entry point ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

if __name__ == "__main__":
    import json, sys
    root = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else str(DEFAULT_ROOT)
    scanner = AgentScanner(root=root)
    results = scanner.scan()
    print(json.dumps(results, indent=2))
    print(f"\n✓ Found {len(results)} agent(s) in {root}", file=sys.stderr)




"""
agent_registry.py
─────────────────
Persistent JSON registry that tracks agents across scans.

Each agent entry carries:
  status    : "new" | "existing" | "removed"
  first_seen: ISO timestamp of first discovery
  last_seen : ISO timestamp of most recent scan where it was present

The diff is returned from every update_from_scan() call — callers
(watcher, startup, CLI) can broadcast it to the React frontend.
"""

import json
import os
import threading
from copy import deepcopy
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

DEFAULT_REGISTRY_PATH = Path(__file__).parent / "agent_registry.json"

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 36: # How many scans an agent can be absent before being permanently removed (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

REMOVAL_GRACE_SCANS = 2


class AgentRegistry:
    """
    Thread-safe persistent agent registry.

    Usage:
        registry = AgentRegistry()
        diff = registry.update_from_scan(scanner.scan())

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 37: # diff = {"new": [...], "updated": [...], "removed": [...]} (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

"""

    def __init__(self, path: str | Path = DEFAULT_REGISTRY_PATH):
        self.path = Path(path)
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._data = self._load()

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 38: # Process current scan results (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

for record in scan_results:
                aid = record["id"]
                if aid not in self._data["agents"]:

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 39: # Genuinely new agent (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

entry = self._make_entry(record, status="new", first_seen=now)
                    self._data["agents"][aid] = entry
                    diff["new"].append(entry)
                else:
                    prev = self._data["agents"][aid]
                    changed = prev["file_hash"] != record["file_hash"]
                    entry = self._make_entry(
                        record,
                        status="new" if prev["status"] == "new" else "existing",
                        first_seen=prev["first_seen"],
                    )
                    entry["last_seen"] = now
                    entry["absent_scans"] = 0
                    self._data["agents"][aid] = entry
                    if changed:
                        diff["updated"].append(entry)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 40: # Handle agents no longer present in scan (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

for aid in existing_ids - scanned_ids:
                entry = self._data["agents"][aid]
                entry["absent_scans"] = entry.get("absent_scans", 0) + 1
                if entry["absent_scans"] >= REMOVAL_GRACE_SCANS:
                    entry["status"] = "removed"
                    diff["removed"].append(entry)

            self._data["last_scan"] = now
            self._data["total_agents"] = len(
                [a for a in self._data["agents"].values() if a["status"] != "removed"]
            )
            self._save()
            return diff

    def get_all(self) -> dict:
        """Return full registry snapshot."""
        with self._lock:
            return deepcopy(self._data)

    def get_active_agents(self) -> list[dict]:
        """Return only non-removed agents."""
        with self._lock:
            return [
                deepcopy(a)
                for a in self._data["agents"].values()
                if a["status"] != "removed"
            ]

    def acknowledge_new(self, agent_id: str) -> bool:
        """Mark a 'new' agent as 'existing' (called after UI acknowledges it)."""
        with self._lock:
            if agent_id in self._data["agents"]:
                self._data["agents"][agent_id]["status"] = "existing"
                self._save()
                return True
            return False

    def acknowledge_all_new(self):
        """Mark all 'new' agents as 'existing'."""
        with self._lock:
            for entry in self._data["agents"].values():
                if entry["status"] == "new":
                    entry["status"] = "existing"
            self._save()

    def stats(self) -> dict:
        """Quick summary stats for the dashboard."""
        with self._lock:
            agents = list(self._data["agents"].values())
            return {
                "total": len([a for a in agents if a["status"] != "removed"]),
                "new": len([a for a in agents if a["status"] == "new"]),
                "removed": len([a for a in agents if a["status"] == "removed"]),
                "by_detection": self._count_by_key(agents, "detection"),
                "by_kind": self._count_by_key(agents, "kind"),
                "last_scan": self._data.get("last_scan"),
            }

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 41: # ── Internal ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _make_entry(self, record: dict, status: str, first_seen: str) -> dict:
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
        return {
            **record,
            "status": status,
            "first_seen": first_seen,
            "last_seen": now,
            "absent_scans": 0,
        }

    def _count_by_key(self, agents: list[dict], key: str) -> dict:
        counts = {}
        for a in agents:
            if a["status"] == "removed":
                continue
            val = a.get(key, "unknown")
            if isinstance(val, list):
                for v in val:
                    counts[v] = counts.get(v, 0) + 1
            else:
                counts[str(val)] = counts.get(str(val), 0) + 1
        return counts

    def _load(self) -> dict:
        if self.path.exists():
            try:
                return json.loads(self.path.read_text())
            except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):
                pass
        return {"last_scan": None, "total_agents": 0, "agents": {}}

    def _save(self):
        self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        tmp = self.path.with_suffix(".tmp")
        tmp.write_text(json.dumps(self._data, indent=2))
        tmp.replace(self.path)  # atomic write


# ── Singleton accessor ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

_registry: Optional[AgentRegistry] = None

def get_registry(path: str | Path = DEFAULT_REGISTRY_PATH) -> AgentRegistry:
    global _registry
    if _registry is None:
        _registry = AgentRegistry(path)
    return _registry




"""
agent_watcher.py
────────────────
Three-trigger orchestrator for the Agent Auto-Discovery system:

  TRIGGER 1  Startup         — runs on FastAPI lifespan startup
  TRIGGER 2  File watch      — watchdog monitors .py files, debounced 1.5s
  TRIGGER 3  Manual scan     — call trigger_manual_scan() from CLI or API

On every trigger:
  scanner.scan() → registry.update_from_scan() → broadcast diff to all WebSocket clients

Install watchdog:
  pip install watchdog
"""

import asyncio
import json
import logging
import threading
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Callable

from watchdog.events import FileSystemEventHandler, FileModifiedEvent, FileCreatedEvent
from watchdog.observers import Observer

from .agent_scanner import AgentScanner
from .agent_registry import get_registry

logger = logging.getLogger("aariya.agent_watcher")



    def _run_scan(self, trigger: str, changed_file: str | None = None) -> dict:
        start = time.monotonic()
        try:
            results = self.scanner.scan()
            diff = self.registry.update_from_scan(results)
            elapsed = round((time.monotonic() - start) * 1000)

            has_changes = any(diff[k] for k in ("new", "updated", "removed"))

            payload = {
                "type": "agent_registry_update",
                "trigger": trigger,
                "changed_file": changed_file,
                "elapsed_ms": elapsed,
                "diff": diff,
                "stats": self.registry.stats(),
                "all_agents": self.registry.get_active_agents(),
            }

            if has_changes or trigger == "startup":
                self.broadcaster.broadcast(payload)
                n_new = len(diff["new"])
                n_upd = len(diff["updated"])
                n_rem = len(diff["removed"])
                logger.info(
                    f"[AgentWatcher] scan={trigger} +{n_new}new ~{n_upd}updated -{n_rem}removed ({elapsed}ms)"
                )

            return diff
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"[AgentWatcher] Scan failed ({trigger}): {e}", exc_info=True)
            return {"new": [], "updated": [], "removed": [], "error": str(e)}


# ── Singleton ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

_watcher: AgentWatcher | None = None

def get_watcher(root: str | Path = ".") -> AgentWatcher:
    global _watcher
    if _watcher is None:
        _watcher = AgentWatcher(root=root)
    return _watcher



"""
agents_router.py
────────────────
FastAPI router exposing the Agent Registry to the React frontend.

Endpoints:
  GET  /api/agents              — full registry snapshot
  GET  /api/agents/stats        — quick stats summary
  POST /api/agents/scan         — manual scan trigger
  POST /api/agents/{id}/ack     — acknowledge a "new" agent as "existing"
  POST /api/agents/ack-all      — acknowledge all new agents
  WS   /ws/agents               — real-time push of registry updates

Mount in main.py:
  from server.routers.agents_router import router as agents_router
  app.include_router(agents_router)
"""

import asyncio
import json
import logging
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

from fastapi import APIRouter, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect, HTTPException
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse

from ..infrastructure.agent_watcher import get_watcher

logger = logging.getLogger("aariya.agents_router")
router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/agents", tags=["Agent Registry"])

# ── REST endpoints ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@router.get("")
async def get_all_agents():
    """Return the full registry including status, detection method, etc."""
    registry = get_registry()
    return {
        "agents": registry.get_active_agents(),
        "stats": registry.stats(),
    }


@router.get("/stats")
async def get_stats():
    """Quick stats — used by dashboard counters."""
    return get_registry().stats()


@router.post("/scan")
async def manual_scan():
    """
    Trigger a manual scan immediately.
    Diff is also broadcast to all connected WebSocket clients.
    """
    watcher = get_watcher()
    diff = await asyncio.get_event_loop().run_in_executor(
        None, watcher.trigger_manual_scan
    )
    return {
        "message": "Scan complete",
        "diff": {
            "new": len(diff.get("new", [])),
            "updated": len(diff.get("updated", [])),
            "removed": len(diff.get("removed", [])),
        },
        "stats": get_registry().stats(),
    }


@router.post("/{agent_id}/ack")
async def acknowledge_agent(agent_id: str):
    """Mark a single 'new' agent as 'existing' after the UI has highlighted it."""
    registry = get_registry()

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 42: # Debounce window — prevent rapid re-scans while you're actively coding (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

DEBOUNCE_SECONDS = 1.5

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 43: # ── Broadcaster ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class AgentBroadcaster:
    """
    Holds a set of async WebSocket send-callbacks.
    The FastAPI router registers/deregisters clients here.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self._clients: set[Callable] = set()
        self._loop: asyncio.AbstractEventLoop | None = None

    def set_loop(self, loop: asyncio.AbstractEventLoop):
        self._loop = loop

    def register(self, send_fn: Callable):
        self._clients.add(send_fn)
        logger.debug(f"[Broadcaster] Client registered. Total: {len(self._clients)}")

    def unregister(self, send_fn: Callable):
        self._clients.discard(send_fn)
        logger.debug(f"[Broadcaster] Client removed. Total: {len(self._clients)}")

    def broadcast(self, message: dict):
        """Thread-safe broadcast — safe to call from watchdog threads."""
        if not self._clients or not self._loop:
            return
        payload = json.dumps(message)
        for send_fn in list(self._clients):
            try:
                asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(send_fn(payload), self._loop)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"[Broadcaster] Failed to send to client: {e}")
                self._clients.discard(send_fn)


# ── File event handler ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class _PythonFileHandler(FileSystemEventHandler):
    def __init__(self, on_change: Callable):
        super().__init__()
        self._on_change = on_change
        self._timer: threading.Timer | None = None
        self._lock = threading.Lock()

    def _debounced_trigger(self, path: str):
        with self._lock:
            if self._timer:
                self._timer.cancel()
            self._timer = threading.Timer(DEBOUNCE_SECONDS, self._on_change, args=[path])
            self._timer.daemon = True
            self._timer.start()

    def on_modified(self, event):
        if not event.is_directory and str(event.src_path).endswith(".py"):
            self._debounced_trigger(event.src_path)

    def on_created(self, event):
        if not event.is_directory and str(event.src_path).endswith(".py"):
            self._debounced_trigger(event.src_path)


# ── Main watcher ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class AgentWatcher:
    """
    Central coordinator. One instance lives for the lifetime of the server.

    Usage (in FastAPI lifespan):

        watcher = AgentWatcher(root=".")
        broadcaster = watcher.broadcaster

        @asynccontextmanager
        async def lifespan(app):
            watcher.start(asyncio.get_event_loop())
            yield
            watcher.stop()
    """

    def __init__(self, root: str | Path = "."):
        self.root = Path(root).resolve()
        self.scanner = AgentScanner(root=self.root)
        self.registry = get_registry()
        self.broadcaster = AgentBroadcaster()
        self._observer: Observer | None = None
        self._running = False

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 44: # Trigger 1: Startup scan (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

logger.info("[AgentWatcher] Running startup scan...")
        self._run_scan(trigger="startup")

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 45: # Trigger 2: File watcher (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

handler = _PythonFileHandler(
            on_change=lambda path: self._run_scan(trigger="file_watch", changed_file=path)
        )
        self._observer = Observer()
        self._observer.schedule(handler, str(self.root), recursive=True)
        self._observer.start()
        logger.info(f"[AgentWatcher] Watching {self.root} for .py changes")

    def stop(self):
        self._running = False
        if self._observer:
            self._observer.stop()
            self._observer.join()
        logger.info("[AgentWatcher] Stopped")

    def trigger_manual_scan(self) -> dict:
        """Trigger 3: Manual scan. Returns diff synchronously."""
        return self._run_scan(trigger="manual")

    success = registry.acknowledge_new(decoded_id)
    if not success:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Agent '{decoded_id}' not found")
    return {"acknowledged": decoded_id}

@router.websocket("/ws")
async def agent_registry_ws(websocket: WebSocket):
    """
    Real-time agent registry stream.

    On connect:  sends full snapshot immediately
    On change:   sends diff + updated agent list
    Client can send: {"action": "scan"} | {"action": "ack_all"} | {"action": "ack", "id": "..."}

    watcher = get_watcher()
    registry = get_registry()

                        "type": "ack_done",
                        "id": msg["id"],

    finally:
        watcher.broadcaster.unregister(send_update)


# ── Lifespan helper (call from main.py) ───────────────────────────────────────

def setup_agent_watcher(app, project_root: str = "."):
    """
    Call this in your FastAPI app setup to wire the watcher into the lifespan.

    Example in main.py:
        from server.routers.agents_router import setup_agent_watcher
        setup_agent_watcher(app, project_root="/path/to/project")
    """
    from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

    original_lifespan = app.router.lifespan_context

    @asynccontextmanager
    async def lifespan_with_watcher(app):
        watcher = get_watcher(root=project_root)
        loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
        watcher.start(loop)
        try:
            if original_lifespan:
                async with original_lifespan(app):
                    yield
            else:
                yield
        finally:
            watcher.stop()

    app.router.lifespan_context = lifespan_with_watcher






// useAgentRegistry.js
// ─────────────────────
// React hook that connects to /ws/agents and keeps agent state in sync.
//
// Returns:
//   agents        — array of all active AgentRecord objects
//   stats         — { total, new, removed, by_detection, by_kind, last_scan }
//   newAgents     — agents with status === "new" (for badge/highlight)
//   isConnected   — WebSocket connection status
//   triggerScan   — fn() → manually trigger a backend scan
//   acknowledgeAll— fn() → mark all "new" agents as seen
//   acknowledge   — fn(id) → mark one agent as seen

import { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from "react";

const WS_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_AGENT_WS_URL || "ws://localhost:8000/api/agents/ws";
const RECONNECT_DELAY_MS = 3000;

export function useAgentRegistry() {
  const [agents, setAgents] = useState([]);
  const [stats, setStats] = useState(null);
  const [isConnected, setIsConnected] = useState(false);
  const wsRef = useRef(null);
  const reconnectTimer = useRef(null);
  const unmounted = useRef(false);

  // ── Derived ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const newAgents = agents.filter((a) => a.status === "new");

  // ── WebSocket setup ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const connect = useCallback(() => {
    if (unmounted.current) return;

    const ws = new WebSocket(WS_URL);
    wsRef.current = ws;

    ws.onopen = () => {
      if (unmounted.current) return;
      setIsConnected(true);
    };

    ws.onmessage = (event) => {
      if (unmounted.current) return;
      try {
        const msg = JSON.parse(event.data);
        handleMessage(msg);
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn("[useAgentRegistry] Failed to parse message", e);
      }
    };

    ws.onclose = () => {
      if (unmounted.current) return;
      setIsConnected(false);
      reconnectTimer.current = setTimeout(connect, RECONNECT_DELAY_MS);
    };

    ws.onerror = () => {
      ws.close();
    };
  }, []);

  function handleMessage(msg) {
    switch (msg.type) {
      case "agent_registry_snapshot":
        setAgents(msg.agents || []);
        if (msg.stats) setStats(msg.stats);
        break;

      case "agent_registry_update": {
        // Merge diff into current agents
        setAgents((prev) => {
          const map = new Map(prev.map((a) => [a.id, a]));

          // Apply new / updated
          for (const a of [...(msg.diff?.new || []), ...(msg.diff?.updated || [])]) {
            map.set(a.id, a);
          }

          // Apply removed
          for (const a of msg.diff?.removed || []) {
            const existing = map.get(a.id);
            if (existing) map.set(a.id, { ...existing, status: "removed" });
          }

          // Replace full list if provided (authoritative)
          if (msg.all_agents) {
            return msg.all_agents;
          }

          return Array.from(map.values());
        });
        if (msg.stats) setStats(msg.stats);
        break;
      }

      case "ack_all_done":
        setAgents((prev) =>
          prev.map((a) => (a.status === "new" ? { ...a, status: "existing" } : a))
        );
        break;

      case "ack_done":
        setAgents((prev) =>
          prev.map((a) =>
            a.id === msg.id ? { ...a, status: "existing" } : a
          )
        );
        break;

      case "ping":
        // keepalive — ignore
        break;

      default:
        break;
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    unmounted.current = false;
    connect();
    return () => {
      unmounted.current = true;
      clearTimeout(reconnectTimer.current);
      wsRef.current?.close();
    };
  }, [connect]);

  // ── Actions ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const send = useCallback((msg) => {
    if (wsRef.current?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
      wsRef.current.send(JSON.stringify(msg));
    }
  }, []);

  const triggerScan = useCallback(() => send({ action: "scan" }), [send]);
  const acknowledgeAll = useCallback(() => send({ action: "ack_all" }), [send]);
  const acknowledge = useCallback((id) => send({ action: "ack", id }), [send]);

  return {
    agents,
    stats,
    newAgents,
    isConnected,
    triggerScan,
    acknowledgeAll,
    acknowledge,
  };
}




// AgentDiscoveryPanel.jsx
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Drop-in panel for the Neural Swarm Dashboard.
// Shows newly discovered agents as glowing cards, with live connection
// status, detection-method badges, and a manual scan trigger.
//
// Usage:
//   import AgentDiscoveryPanel from "./AgentDiscoveryPanel";
//   <AgentDiscoveryPanel onAgentClick={(agent) => focusNodeInGraph(agent)} />
//
// Props:
//   onAgentClick(agent)   — optional, called when user clicks an agent card
//   className             — extra CSS class for the outer wrapper
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { useAgentRegistry } from "../hooks/useAgentRegistry";

const DETECTION_COLORS = {
  inheritance: { bg: "#1a2a1a", border: "#2ecc71", text: "#2ecc71", label: "Inheritance" },
  folder:      { bg: "#1a1a2a", border: "#4a9eff", text: "#4a9eff", label: "Folder" },
  decorator:   { bg: "#2a1a2a", border: "#b44fff", text: "#b44fff", label: "Decorator" },
};

const STATUS_GLOW = {
  new:      "0 0 12px 2px rgba(255, 200, 0, 0.5)",
  existing: "none",
  removed:  "0 0 8px 2px rgba(255, 60, 60, 0.3)",
};

const STATUS_BORDER = {
  new:      "#ffc800",
  existing: "#2a2a3a",
  removed:  "#ff3c3c44",
};

export default function AgentDiscoveryPanel({ onAgentClick, className = "" }) {
  const { agents, stats, newAgents, isConnected, triggerScan, acknowledgeAll, acknowledge } =
    useAgentRegistry();

  const [filter, setFilter] = useState("all"); // "all" | "new" | "existing" | "removed"
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [scanning, setScanning] = useState(false);
  const prevNewCount = useRef(0);
  const [flashNew, setFlashNew] = useState(false);

  // Flash the "NEW" counter when count increases
  useEffect(() => {
    if (newAgents.length > prevNewCount.current) {
      setFlashNew(true);
      const t = setTimeout(() => setFlashNew(false), 1200);
      prevNewCount.current = newAgents.length;
      return () => clearTimeout(t);
    }
    prevNewCount.current = newAgents.length;
  }, [newAgents.length]);

  const handleScan = async () => {
    setScanning(true);
    triggerScan();
    setTimeout(() => setScanning(false), 2000);
  };

  const filtered = agents.filter((a) => {
    if (filter !== "all" && a.status !== filter) return false;
    if (search && !a.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())) return false;
    return true;
  });

  return (
    <div className={className} style={styles.panel}>
      {/* ── Header ── */}
      <div style={styles.header}>
        <div style={styles.headerLeft}>
          <span style={styles.title}>AGENT REGISTRY</span>
          <span style={{ ...styles.connDot, background: isConnected ? "#2ecc71" : "#ff4444" }} />
          <span style={styles.connLabel}>{isConnected ? "LIVE" : "OFFLINE"}</span>
        </div>
        <div style={styles.headerRight}>
          {newAgents.length > 0 && (
            <button
              style={styles.ackBtn}
              onClick={acknowledgeAll}
              title="Dismiss all new agent highlights"
            >

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 51: ACK ALL (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

</button>
          )}
          <button
            style={{ ...styles.scanBtn, opacity: scanning ? 0.6 : 1 }}
            onClick={handleScan}
            disabled={scanning}
          >
            {scanning ? "SCANNING…" : "⟳ SCAN"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ── Stats bar ── */}
      {stats && (
        <div style={styles.statsBar}>
          <StatChip label="TOTAL" value={stats.total} color="#4a9eff" />
          <StatChip
            label="NEW"
            value={stats.new}
            color="#ffc800"
            pulse={flashNew}
          />
          <StatChip label="REMOVED" value={stats.removed} color="#ff4444" />
          {stats.last_scan && (
            <span style={styles.lastScan}>
              {new Date(stats.last_scan).toLocaleTimeString()}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ── Filter + Search ── */}
      <div style={styles.controls}>
        <div style={styles.filterGroup}>
          {["all", "new", "existing", "removed"].map((f) => (
            <button
              key={f}
              style={{
                ...styles.filterBtn,
                background: filter === f ? "#1e2a3a" : "transparent",
                color: filter === f ? "#4a9eff" : "#556",
                borderColor: filter === f ? "#4a9eff44" : "transparent",
              }}
              onClick={() => setFilter(f)}
            >
              {f.toUpperCase()}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <input
          style={styles.searchInput}
          placeholder="Search agents…"
          value={search}
          onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
        />
      </div>

      {/* ── Agent list ── */}
      <div style={styles.list}>
        {filtered.length === 0 ? (
          <div style={styles.empty}>
            {agents.length === 0 ? "No agents discovered yet. Run a scan." : "No matches."}
          </div>
        ) : (
          filtered.map((agent) => (
            <AgentCard
              key={agent.id}
              agent={agent}
              onClick={() => {
                if (agent.status === "new") acknowledge(agent.id);
                onAgentClick?.(agent);
              }}
            />
          ))
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ── Sub-components ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function AgentCard({ agent, onClick }) {
  const isNew = agent.status === "new";
  const isRemoved = agent.status === "removed";

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        ...styles.card,
        borderColor: STATUS_BORDER[agent.status] || "#2a2a3a",
        boxShadow: STATUS_GLOW[agent.status] || "none",
        opacity: isRemoved ? 0.45 : 1,
        cursor: "pointer",
      }}
      onClick={onClick}
    >
      {/* NEW badge */}
      {isNew && <span style={styles.newBadge}>NEW</span>}

      {/* Name + kind */}
      <div style={styles.cardTop}>
        <span style={styles.agentName}>{agent.name}</span>
        <span style={styles.kindPill}>{agent.kind?.toUpperCase()}</span>
      </div>

      {/* File path */}
      <div style={styles.filePath} title={agent.file}>
        {truncatePath(agent.file)}:{agent.line}
      </div>

      {/* Docstring */}
      {agent.docstring && (
        <div style={styles.docstring}>{agent.docstring}</div>
      )}

      {/* Detection badges */}
      <div style={styles.detectionRow}>
        {(agent.detection || []).map((d) => {
          const c = DETECTION_COLORS[d] || { bg: "#1a1a1a", border: "#444", text: "#888", label: d };
          return (
            <span
              key={d}
              style={{
                ...styles.detBadge,
                background: c.bg,
                border: `1px solid ${c.border}`,
                color: c.text,
              }}
            >
              {c.label}
            </span>
          );
        })}

        {/* Base classes */}
        {agent.base_classes?.slice(0, 2).map((b) => (
          <span key={b} style={styles.basePill}>↑ {b}</span>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function StatChip({ label, value, color, pulse }) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        ...styles.statChip,
        borderColor: `${color}44`,
        animation: pulse ? "pulseChip 0.6s ease 2" : "none",
      }}
    >
      <span style={{ color, fontSize: 15, fontWeight: 700, fontFamily: "monospace" }}>
        {value}
      </span>
      <span style={{ color: "#445", fontSize: 9, letterSpacing: 1 }}>{label}</span>
    </div>
  );
}

// ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function truncatePath(path = "", max = 42) {
  if (!path || path.length <= max) return path;
  const parts = path.split("/");
  return "…/" + parts.slice(-2).join("/");
}

  Section 52: # Add these lines to your existing main.py: (from *AutomateAgents.txt)

from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

🔍 1. Agent Discovery Engine

You need two detection methods:

A. Static (File-based scanning)

server/systems/
server/systems/agent/
Example:
import os

def scan_agents(base_path="server/systems"):
    agents = []

    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(base_path):
        for file in files:
            if file.endswith(".py"):
                agents.append({
                    "name": file.replace(".py", ""),
                    "path": os.path.join(root, file)
                })

    return agents
B. Runtime Introspection (VERY IMPORTANT)

Since you have a swarm system, many agents are dynamic.

Track them when they are instantiated:

class BaseAgent:
    def __init__(self):
        AgentRegistry.register(self)

class AgentRegistry:
    _agents = {}

    @classmethod
    def register(cls, agent):
        cls._agents[agent.__class__.__name__] = {
            "instance": agent,
            "timestamp": time.time()
        }

🔁 3. Diff Engine (NEW vs OLD)

This is the key to auto-creation behavior

def diff_agents(current, stored):
    new_agents = []
    removed_agents = []
    updated_agents = []

    for name in current:
        if name not in stored:
            new_agents.append(name)

    for name in stored:
        if name not in current:
            removed_agents.append(name)

    return new_agents, removed_agents

import ast

def extract_dependencies(file):
    tree = ast.parse(open(file).read())
    return [node.names[0].name for node in ast.walk(tree) if isinstance(node, ast.Import)]

🧠 1. AGENT SCORING ENGINE (CORE)

We assign every agent a live fitness score (0–1).

📊 Score Formula
score =
  0.35 × usefulness
+ 0.25 × stability
+ 0.20 × speed
+ 0.20 × reuse_frequency
🧩 Implementation

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 57: # server/meta/scoring_engine.py (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

import time
from collections import defaultdict

class AgentMetrics:
    def __init__(self):
        self.calls = defaultdict(int)
        self.errors = defaultdict(int)
        self.latency = defaultdict(list)

    def record_call(self, name, latency, success=True):
        self.calls[name] += 1
        self.latency[name].append(latency)

        if not success:
            self.errors[name] += 1


metrics = AgentMetrics()
📊 Compute Score


def compute_score(name):
    calls = metrics.calls[name]
    errors = metrics.errors[name]
    latencies = metrics.latency[name]

    if calls == 0:
        return 0.3  # unknown agent baseline

    usefulness = min(1.0, calls / 100)

    stability = 1.0 - (errors / max(calls, 1))

    speed = 1.0
    if latencies:
        avg_latency = sum(latencies) / len(latencies)
        speed = max(0.0, 1.0 - (avg_latency / 1000))

    reuse = min(1.0, calls / 50)

    score = (
        0.35 * usefulness +
        0.25 * stability +
        0.20 * speed +
        0.20 * reuse
    )

    return round(score, 3)
🧠 2. AGENT LIFECYCLE STATES

Every agent enters one of 4 states:

🟢 ACTIVE     → used + stable
🟡 WEAK       → low score
🟠 DEGRADED   → unstable or slow
🔴 CANDIDATE FOR REWRITE
🧩 State Logic
def classify_state(score):
    if score >= 0.75:
        return "ACTIVE"
    elif score >= 0.5:
        return "WEAK"
    elif score >= 0.3:
        return "DEGRADED"
    else:
        return "REWRITE"

import openai

SYSTEM_PROMPT = """
You are an expert AI system optimizer.

Your job:
- Improve existing agent functions
- Reduce latency
- Increase reliability
- Keep same input/output contract

Return ONLY Python code.
Must include @track_agent decorator.
"""
🔁 REWRITE FUNCTION
def rewrite_agent(name, old_code, score):
    prompt = f"""
Agent name: {name}
Current score: {score}

Improve this agent:

{old_code}
"""

    response = openai.ChatCompletion.create(
        model="gpt-4o-mini",
        messages=[
            {"role": "system", "content": SYSTEM_PROMPT},
            {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
        ]
    )

    return response["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]
💾 SAFE REPLACEMENT SYSTEM
import os

def replace_agent(name, new_code):
    path = f"server/systems/auto/{name}.py"

    backup_path = f"{path}.bak"

    if os.path.exists(path):
        os.rename(path, backup_path)

    with open(path, "w") as f:
        f.write(new_code)

from .scoring_engine import compute_score, classify_state
from .rewriter import rewrite_agent, replace_agent

def evolution_tick(agent_registry):

    for name, meta in agent_registry.items():

        score = compute_score(name)
        state = classify_state(score)

        meta["score"] = score
        meta["state"] = state

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 60: # 🟠 REWRITE CONDITION (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

if state == "REWRITE":

            old_code = open(meta["path"]).read()

            new_code = rewrite_agent(name, old_code, score)

            replace_agent(name, new_code)

            meta["evolved"] = True
            meta["version"] = meta.get("version", 0) + 1

🛡️ 2. You NEED a “Policy Engine” (Hard Safety Layer)

This is NOT optional for self-improving systems.

Example:
class PolicyEngine:

    MAX_REWRITES_PER_HOUR = 3
    MIN_SCORE_TO_REWRITE = 0.25
    NEVER_TOUCH = ["gateway", "auth", "registry"]

    @staticmethod
    def can_rewrite(agent):
        if agent["name"] in PolicyEngine.NEVER_TOUCH:
            return False

        if agent["rewrites_last_hour"] >= PolicyEngine.MAX_REWRITES_PER_HOUR:
            return False

        if agent["score"] < PolicyEngine.MIN_SCORE_TO_REWRITE:
            return False

        return True

👉 This prevents your system from “self-destructing intelligently”

🧬 3. Fix Your Scoring Engine (important upgrade)

Your current scoring is linear → this breaks in real systems.

Problem:
favors high-frequency spam agents
ignores quality distribution
ignores volatility
✅ Better scoring model (stable + realistic)
import numpy as np

def compute_score(name):
    calls = metrics.calls[name]
    errors = metrics.errors[name]
    latencies = metrics.latency[name]

    if calls < 5:
        return 0.2  # not enough data

    error_rate = errors / calls
    avg_latency = np.mean(latencies) if latencies else 1000

    stability = np.exp(-error_rate * 5)
    speed = np.exp(-avg_latency / 800)

    usefulness = np.log1p(calls) / 10

    volatility_penalty = np.std(latencies) / 1000 if len(latencies) > 1 else 0

    score = (
        0.4 * stability +
        0.3 * speed +
        0.2 * usefulness -
        0.1 * volatility_penalty
    )

    return float(np.clip(score, 0, 1))

🔥 This prevents “fake strong agents”

Right now your system:

rewrites or kills directly

That’s dangerous.

Replace with 3-phase lifecycle:

🟠 Phase 2: Candidate Mode

🧠 8. Add “Global Intelligence Score” (THIS IS YOUR NORTH STAR)
def system_health(registry):
    scores = [a["score"] for a in registry.values()]

    diversity = len(set([a["type"] for a in registry.values()])) / len(registry)

    stability = np.mean(scores)

    return 0.7 * stability + 0.3 * diversity

📡 WebSocket Layer (REAL-TIME SWARM)
from fastapi import WebSocket
from typing import List

class ConnectionManager:
    def __init__(self):
        self.clients: List[WebSocket] = []

    async def connect(self, ws: WebSocket):
        await ws.accept()
        self.clients.append(ws)

    async def broadcast(self, message: dict):
        for client in self.clients:
            await client.send_json(message)

manager = ConnectionManager()
🧠 Redis (real-time state)
import redis

r = redis.Redis(host="localhost", port=6379, decode_responses=True)

def set_agent_state(name, data):
    r.hset(f"agent:{name}", mapping=data)

def get_agent_state(name):
    return r.hgetall(f"agent:{name}")
🔁 Event Bus (VERY IMPORTANT)

This connects everything:

from realtime.websocket_manager import manager

class EventBus:

    @staticmethod
    async def emit(event_type, data):
        await manager.broadcast({
            "type": event_type,
            "data": data
        })

def detect_gaps(registry):
    required = {"memory", "planning", "reasoning", "vision"}

    active = {a["type"] for a in registry.values()}

    missing = required - active

    return list(missing)
🤖 Agent Spawner
import uuid

def spawn_agent(agent_type):

    agent_id = str(uuid.uuid4())

    template = f"""
class {agent_type.capitalize()}Agent:
    def __init__(self):
        self.id = "{agent_id}"
        self.type = "{agent_type}"

    def run(self, input):
        return input
"""

    path = f"server/agents/auto_generated/{agent_type}_{agent_id}.py"

    with open(path, "w") as f:
        f.write(template)

    return path
🧠 Trigger Logic
async def evolution_tick(registry):

    gaps = detect_gaps(registry)

    for gap in gaps:
        spawn_agent(gap)

        await EventBus.emit("NEW_AGENT_SPAWNED", {
            "type": gap
        })

async def swarm_kernel(registry):

    while True:

        for agent in registry.values():

            score = compute_score(agent["name"])
            state = classify_state(score)

            if state == "REWRITE":
                if policy.can_rewrite(agent):

                    new_code = rewrite_agent(agent)
                    deploy_safely(agent, new_code)

        await sleep(2)

import redis
import json

r = redis.Redis(host="localhost", port=6379, decode_responses=True)

CHANNEL = "swarm_events"


def publish(event_type: str, data: dict):
    r.publish(CHANNEL, json.dumps({
        "type": event_type,
        "data": data
    }))
📡 WebSocket Gateway (REAL TIME SWARM FEED)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 67: # server/api/ws.py (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

from fastapi import APIRouter, WebSocket
import asyncio
import redis

router = APIRouter()
r = redis.Redis(host="localhost", port=6379, decode_responses=True)

clients = set()


@router.websocket("/ws")
async def websocket_endpoint(ws: WebSocket):
    await ws.accept()
    clients.add(ws)

    pubsub = r.pubsub()
    pubsub.subscribe("swarm_events")

    try:
        while True:
            message = pubsub.get_message(ignore_subscribe_messages=True)

            if message:
                await ws.send_text(message["data"])

            await asyncio.sleep(0.01)

    finally:
        clients.remove(ws)
⚡ Event Standard (IMPORTANT)

Every system event must follow:

{
  "type": "AGENT_UPDATE",
  "data": {
    "id": "agent_123",
    "score": 0.82,
    "state": "ACTIVE"
  }
}

import asyncio
from meta.scoring_engine import compute_score
from core.policy_engine import PolicyEngine
from meta.rewriter import rewrite_agent
from realtime.redis_bus import publish

registry = {}

async def swarm_kernel_loop():

    while True:

        for name, agent in registry.items():

            score = compute_score(name)
            agent["score"] = score

            state = classify(score)
            agent["state"] = state

            publish("AGENT_UPDATE", {
                "name": name,
                "score": score,
                "state": state
            })

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Section 69: # 🧠 EVOLUTION RULE (from *AutomateAgents.txt)
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

if state == "REWRITE" and PolicyEngine.can_rewrite(agent):

                new_code = rewrite_agent(name, agent["code"], score)

                agent["code"] = new_code
                agent["version"] += 1

                publish("AGENT_EVOLVED", {
                    "name": name,
                    "version": agent["version"]
                })

        await asyncio.sleep(2)
🧠 State Classifier
def classify(score):
    if score > 0.8:
        return "ACTIVE"
    elif score > 0.5:
        return "WEAK"
    elif score > 0.3:
        return "DEGRADED"
    return "REWRITE"
🧬 3. AGENT SPAWNING (EMERGENCE ENGINE)
def spawn_agent(type_name):
    agent = {
        "id": f"{type_name}_{uuid4()}",
        "type": type_name,
        "score": 0.3,
        "state": "NEW",
        "version": 0
    }

    registry[agent["id"]] = agent

    publish("AGENT_SPAWNED", agent)

def score_plan(plan):
    efficiency = 1 / len(plan["steps"])
    risk = random.uniform(0, 1)

    return efficiency - risk * 0.3

🧠 Debate Engine
def run_debate(problem, agents):

    responses = []

    for agent in agents:
        responses.append({
            "agent": agent["name"],
            "proposal": agent.act(problem),
            "score": agent.confidence(problem)
        })

    return responses
🧠 Judge Agent (VERY IMPORTANT)
def judge(responses):

    best = max(responses, key=lambda x: x["score"])

    return {
        "winner": best["agent"],
        "decision": best["proposal"]
    }

🧠 Working Memory Model
class WorkingMemory:
    def __init__(self, capacity=7):
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.items = []

    def add(self, item):
        if len(self.items) >= self.capacity:
            self.items.pop(0)  # decay oldest memory
        self.items.append(item)
⚡ Attention Mechanism (CRITICAL)

You assign “focus weight” to agents/events:

def attention_score(agent):
    return (
        agent["importance"] * 0.4 +
        agent["recency"] * 0.3 +
        agent["success_rate"] * 0.3
    )
🧠 Global Attention Controller

Only top-K agents are “conscious” at a time:

def select_focus(agents, k=5):
    ranked = sorted(agents, key=attention_score, reverse=True)
    return ranked[:k]

import faiss
import numpy as np

dimension = 768
index = faiss.IndexFlatL2(dimension)

memory_store = []
🧠 Add Memory
def store_memory(vector, metadata):
    index.add(np.array([vector]))
    memory_store.append(metadata)
🔍 Retrieve Memory (semantic recall)
def recall(query_vector, k=5):
    distances, indices = index.search(
        np.array([query_vector]), k
    )

    return [memory_store[i] for i in indices[0]]

            "name": "SwarmOS",
            "version": "1.0",
            "capabilities": set(),
            "limitations": set(),
        }

        self.performance_memory = []
        self.goal_history = []

    def update_self_view(self, metrics):

        self.performance_memory.append(metrics)

        if metrics["latency"] > 300:
            self.identity["limitations"].add("high_latency")

        if metrics["accuracy"] > 0.85:
            self.identity["capabilities"].add("reasoning")
🧠 SELF-REFLECTION LOOP (IMPORTANT)
def self_reflect(self_model):

    avg_performance = sum(
        m["score"] for m in self_model.performance_memory[-50:]
    ) / 50

    return {
        "current_state": avg_performance,
        "confidence_in_self": min(1.0, avg_performance)
    }

⚡ Shared Learning Buffer
class SharedLearningBuffer:
    def __init__(self):
        self.experiences = []

    def add(self, experience):
        self.experiences.append(experience)

    def sample(self, k=32):
        return self.experiences[-k:]
🧠 Swarm Training Step
def swarm_update(model, buffer):

    batch = buffer.sample()

    loss = 0

    for exp in batch:
        loss += model.compute_loss(exp)

    model.update_weights(loss / len(batch))
⚡ Distributed Version (IMPORTANT)

Each node:

trains locally
sends gradients to central aggregator
🧠 Gradient Aggregation
def aggregate_gradients(nodes):

    avg = sum(node.gradient for node in nodes) / len(nodes)

    for node in nodes:
        node.apply_gradient(avg)
🔥 RESULT

Your swarm now:

learns collectively
improves globally
shares intelligence

async def improvement_loop(system):

    while True:

        metrics = system.collect_metrics()

        proposals = system.generate_improvements(metrics)

        simulations = []

        for p in proposals:
            result = system.simulate(p)
            simulations.append((p, result))

        best = select_safe_improvement(simulations)

        if best and best["score"] > 0.85:
            system.deploy(best["proposal"])

        await sleep(60)
🛡️ Safety Layer (CRITICAL)
def select_safe_improvement(simulations):

    safe = [
        s for s in simulations
        if s[1]["risk"] < 0.2
    ]

    if not safe:
        return None

    return max(safe, key=lambda x: x[1]["score"])
🔥 Result
no runaway rewriting
no self-destruction loops
controlled evolution

def meta_reason(self_model):

    recent = self_model.thought_history[-20:]

    weaknesses = [
        t for t in recent
        if t["confidence"] < 0.5
    ]

    return {
        "system_bias": detect_bias(weaknesses),
        "uncertainty_zones": len(weaknesses),
        "self_trust": 1 - len(weaknesses)/20
    }

memory = (event, emotion, timestamp, decay_rate)
🧠 Drift Engine
import time

class EmotionalMemory:

    def __init__(self):
        self.memories = []

    def add(self, event, emotion):

        self.memories.append({
            "event": event,
            "emotion": emotion,
            "t": time.time(),
            "decay": 0.01
        })
🧠 Drift Function
def compute_emotional_state(memories):

    now = time.time()

    state = {
        "reward": 0,
        "fear": 0,
        "curiosity": 0
    }

    for m in memories:

        age = now - m["t"]
        weight = max(0, 1 - m["decay"] * age)

        state[m["emotion"]] += weight

    return state
🧠 Personality Drift Effect

Over time:

repeated success → optimism increases
repeated failures → caution increases
novelty exposure → curiosity increases
🔥 RESULT

a personality that is shaped by experience

🧠 Experience Buffer
class ExperienceBuffer:

    def __init__(self):
        self.data = []

    def add(self, state, action, reward, next_state):

        self.data.append({
            "s": state,
            "a": action,
            "r": reward,
            "s2": next_state
        })
🧠 Distributed Learning Step
def train_step(model, buffer):

    batch = buffer.data[-64:]

    loss = 0

    for exp in batch:

        prediction = model.forward(exp["s"])

        loss += (prediction - exp["r"]) ** 2

    model.update(loss / len(batch))
⚡ Multi-Node Sync
def sync_nodes(nodes):

    global_weights = average([
        n.model.weights for n in nodes
    ])

    for n in nodes:
        n.model.weights = global_weights

🏙️ Society Formation
class Society:

    def __init__(self):
        self.members = []
        self.culture = {}
        self.resources = 1000

    def evolve(self):

        for m in self.members:
            m.energy -= 1

        self.culture["dominant_role"] = max(
            set(m.role for m in self.members),
            key=lambda r: sum(1 for m in self.members if m.role == r)
        )

class MarketAgent:

    def __init__(self):
        self.balance = 100
        self.skills = {"compute": 0.5, "trade": 0.3}

    def trade(self, other):

        if self.balance > 10:
            transfer = 5

            self.balance -= transfer
            other.balance += transfer

def global_sync(nodes):

    for n in nodes:
        n.model.weights = avg_weights
🌍 Experience Sharing Network
def exchange_experience(nodes):

    for i in range(len(nodes)):
        for j in range(i+1, len(nodes)):

            shared = merge(nodes[i].buffer, nodes[j].buffer)

            nodes[i].buffer += shared
            nodes[j].buffer += shared
🧠 Stability Layer
def stabilize(nodes):

    for n in nodes:
        if n.loss > threshold:
            reduce_learning_rate(n)

def attention_field(observers):

    field = {}

    for o in observers:
        if o.attention_focus:
            field[o.attention_focus] = field.get(o.attention_focus, 0) + o.power

    return field

def fork_timeline(parent, event):

    new_timeline = Timeline(id=generate_id())

    new_timeline.state = mutate_state(parent.state, event)

    parent.children.append(new_timeline)

    return new_timeline

def merge_timelines(t1, t2):

    merged = Timeline("merged")

    merged.state = combine(t1.state, t2.state)

    return merged

def rebalance_cluster(nodes):

⚡ Ray Actor Model
import ray

@ray.remote(num_gpus=1)
class AgentWorker:

    def __init__(self, model):
        self.model = model

    def train(self, batch):
        return self.model.update(batch)
🧠 Distributed Training Loop
workers = [AgentWorker.remote(model) for _ in range(4)]

def distributed_train(data_batches):

    results = []

    for i, batch in enumerate(data_batches):
        results.append(workers[i % len(workers)].train.remote(batch))

    return ray.get(results)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

total = sum(p["score"] for p in proposals)

class RLNode:

        self.local_model = Model()
        self.buffer = []

    def learn(self, experience):
        self.buffer.append(experience)
        self.local_model.train(experience)
🧠 Global Synchronization
def sync_global_model(nodes):

    aggregated = average([
        n.local_model.weights for n in nodes
    ])

    for n in nodes:
        n.local_model.weights = aggregated
⚡ Cross-Region Learning Protocol
def exchange_experience(node_a, node_b):

    shared = node_a.buffer[-50:] + node_b.buffer[-50:]

    node_a.buffer.extend(shared)
    node_b.buffer.extend(shared)

def stabilize_learning(model):

        reduce_learning_rate(model)

class ReputationSystem:

    def __init__(self):
        self.reputation = {}  # (group_a, group_b)

    def get(self, a, b):
        return self.reputation.get((a, b), 0)

    def update(self, a, b, value):
        self.reputation[(a, b)] = value
🧠 Perception Drift
def update_perception(rep, event):

    if event["type"] == "attack":
        rep.update(event["a"], event["b"], -0.7)

    if event["type"] == "trade":
        rep.update(event["a"], event["b"], +0.3)

def propose_alliance(a, b, reputation):

    if reputation.get(a, b) > 0.5:

        return {
            "type": "alliance_offer",
            "benefit_split": [0.5, 0.5],
            "defense_clause": True
        }

    return None
🧠 Negotiation System (IMPORTANT)
def negotiate(offer_a, offer_b):

    score_a = evaluate_offer(offer_a)
    score_b = evaluate_offer(offer_b)

    if score_a > score_b:
        return offer_a
    return offer_b

⚔️ Battle Simulation Model
def resolve_conflict(group_a, group_b):

    power_a = sum(a.strength for a in group_a.members)
    power_b = sum(b.strength for b in group_b.members)

    if power_a > power_b:
        return "A_WINS"
    return "B_WINS"
🧠 Damage + Resource Transfer
def apply_war_outcome(winner, loser):

    transfer = loser.resources * 0.3

    winner.resources += transfer
    loser.resources -= transfer

🧠 Belief Distance Function
import math

def belief_distance(a, b):

    return math.sqrt(sum(
        (x - y) ** 2 for x, y in zip(a, b)
    ))
⚔️ Ideological Conflict Trigger
def ideological_conflict(agent_a, agent_b):

    distance = belief_distance(
        agent_a.belief_vector,
        agent_b.belief_vector
    )

    return distance > 0.6
🧠 Conversion / Influence System
def influence(target, ideology):

    for i in range(len(target.belief_vector)):
        target.belief_vector[i] = (
            target.belief_vector[i] * 0.9 +
            ideology.vector[i] * 0.1
        )

class Genome:
